@@ -0,0 +1,92 @@
+"""Dependências v2.11 (captura READ ONLY de produção 02/11, SHA-256 6eed148a…30cc): 15 relações (294 colunas, 120
+constraints), 10 funções (8 nomes, overloads public/platform_private), a view _src_vw_esteira_laboratorio e 2 sequências
+(sem last_value). Sem linhas de dados.
+
+Integração sem stub: as relações entram na MESMA via das extras reais (extras_schema.X) — colunas/tipos/defaults/NOT NULL
+e PK/UNIQUE/CHECK/FK reais; enums novos vêm da captura de enums. accounts_payable já estava nas extras e é idêntica
+(colunas e constraints) — não é duplicada, a igualdade é verificada aqui. Flags de RLS/policies/gatilhos/ACL das 15
+relações NÃO foram capturados: as relações são marcadas `rls_unknown` e nenhuma flag é inventada.
+Sequências: parâmetros reais; max_value chega como float JSON (9.223372036854776e18) = máximo de bigint.
+"""
+import hashlib, json
+from pathlib import Path
+import extras_schema as XS
+
+PATH = XS.ROOT / "docs/auditorias/evidencias/catalogo-exclusivo-tenant-20261101/captura-v211-dependencias-20261102/fechamento-v211-dependencias-20261102.json"
+SHA = "6eed148aa04283c13d8bd99962bf0ed9781ceebc8af5602aa77b1a2c489030cc"
+assert hashlib.sha256(PATH.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == SHA, "captura v2.11 divergente"
+D = json.loads(PATH.read_text())
+assert D["source"].startswith("production READ ONLY swzpzcwiasxmjutrmvkm")
+assert (len(D["columns"]), len(D["constraints"]), len(D["functions"]), len(D["views"]), len(D["sequences"])) == (294, 120, 10, 1, 2)
+
+KIND = (("PRIMARY KEY", "p"), ("UNIQUE", "u"), ("FOREIGN KEY", "f"), ("CHECK", "c"), ("EXCLUDE", "x"))
+
+
+def _kind(defn):
+    for pre, k in KIND:
+        if defn.startswith(pre): return k
+    raise AssertionError("tipo de constraint desconhecido: " + defn[:60])
+
+
+def merge():
+    """Acrescenta as relações novas a XS.X (idempotente). Devolve resumo."""
+    if XS.X.get("_v211"): return XS.X["_v211"]
+    have = {(r["nspname"], r["relname"]) for r in XS.X["relations"]}
+    tabs = []
+    for c in D["columns"]:
+        k = (c["schema"], c["table"])
+        if k not in tabs: tabs.append(k)
+    overlap = [k for k in tabs if k in have]
+    for s, t in overlap:   # igualdade exigida (default None≡'')
+        old = [(c["column_name"], c["type_sql"], c["default_expression"] or "", c["not_null"])
+               for c in sorted(XS.X["columns"], key=lambda c: c["position"]) if (c["schema_name"], c["table_name"]) == (s, t)]
+        new = [(c["name"], c["type"], c["default"] or "", c["not_null"]) for c in D["columns"] if (c["schema"], c["table"]) == (s, t)]
+        assert old == new, f"{s}.{t}: colunas divergentes entre capturas"
+        oc = {c["name"]: c["definition"] for c in XS.X["constraints"] if (c["schema_name"], c["table_name"]) == (s, t)}
+        nc = {c["name"]: c["definition"] for c in D["constraints"] if (c["schema"], c["table"]) == (s, t)}
+        assert oc == nc, f"{s}.{t}: constraints divergentes entre capturas"
+    pos = {}
+    for c in D["columns"]:
+        k = (c["schema"], c["table"])
+        if k in overlap: continue
+        pos[k] = pos.get(k, 0) + 1
+        XS.X["columns"].append(dict(schema_name=k[0], table_name=k[1], column_name=c["name"], type_sql=c["type"],
+                                    default_expression=c["default"] or None, identity=c["identity"], generated=c["generated"],
+                                    not_null=c["not_null"], position=pos[k]))
+    for c in D["constraints"]:
+        k = (c["schema"], c["table"])
+        if k in overlap: continue
+        XS.X["constraints"].append(dict(name=c["name"], type=_kind(c["definition"]), validated="NOT VALID" not in c["definition"],
+                                        definition=c["definition"].replace(" NOT VALID", ""), table_name=k[1], schema_name=k[0]))
+    for k in tabs:
+        if k not in overlap:
+            XS.X["relations"].append(dict(oid=None, nspname=k[0], relkind="r", relname=k[1], relrowsecurity=None,
+                                          relforcerowsecurity=None, rls_unknown=True))
+    XS.X["_v211"] = dict(new_relations=[f"{a}.{b}" for a, b in tabs if (a, b) not in overlap],
+                         identical_overlap=[f"{a}.{b}" for a, b in overlap])
+    return XS.X["_v211"]
+
+
+def sequences_sql():
+    out = []
+    for s in D["sequences"]:
+        mx = "NO MAXVALUE" if s["max_value"] >= 9.2e18 else f"MAXVALUE {int(s['max_value'])}"
+        out.append(f"CREATE SEQUENCE IF NOT EXISTS {XS.fq(s['schemaname'], s['sequencename'])} AS {s['data_type']} "
+                   f"INCREMENT BY {s['increment_by']} MINVALUE {s['min_value']} {mx} START WITH {s['start_value']} "
+                   f"CACHE {s['cache_size']} {'CYCLE' if s['cycle'] else 'NO CYCLE'};")
+    return out
+
+
+def view_sql():
+    v = D["views"][0]
+    return v["name"], f"CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW {XS.fq(v['schema'], v['name'])} AS {v['definition']}"
+
+
+def functions():
+    return [dict(schema=f["schema"], name=f["name"], args=f["identity_args"], definition=f["definition"], md5=f["md5"])
+            for f in D["functions"]]
+
+
+def md5_sql(f):
+    return (f"SELECT md5(pg_get_functiondef(p.oid)) FROM pg_proc p WHERE p.proname='{f['name']}' "
+            f"AND p.pronamespace=to_regnamespace('{f['schema']}') AND pg_get_function_identity_arguments(p.oid)='{f['args']}'")

+"""Efeitos dos gatilhos/ACL/RLS REAIS da captura v2.12 (nada suspenso; cada cenário em transação com ROLLBACK).
+Auth continua sintética (as_user) — não é prova JWT hospedada."""
+import harness as H
+
+KIND = "PG_DISPOSABLE_REAL_SCHEMA_V212_GUARDS_SYNTHETIC_AUTH"
+
+
+def run(pg, q, check, as_user, val):
+    c = lambda cid, scen, exp, act: check(cid, scen, exp, act, kind=KIND)
+    ARB, FSF, UA, UF = H.ARB, H.FSF, H.UA, H.UF
+    FI_A, FI_F, CFS_A, CFS_F = H.U(51), H.U(53), H.U(70), H.U(71)
+    cnt = lambda: q(f"SELECT count(*) FROM public.fiscal_invoices WHERE id='{FI_A}'")
+
+    r = pg.sql(f"BEGIN; DELETE FROM public.fiscal_invoices WHERE id='{FI_A}'; ROLLBACK;", ok=False)
+    pg.sql("ROLLBACK;", ok=False)
+    c("V212-G1", "DELETE de nota fiscal pelo dono do banco: gatilhos reais recusam e a linha permanece",
+      "ERR|1", ("ERR" if r.startswith("ERR") else "OK") + "|" + cnt())
+    H.V212_DETAIL = {"G1": r[:300]}
+
+    r = pg.sql(f"BEGIN; INSERT INTO public.fiscal_invoices(organization_id,invoice_type,company_settings_id,created_by) "
+               f"VALUES ('{ARB}','NFE','{CFS_F}','{UA}'); ROLLBACK;", ok=False)
+    pg.sql("ROLLBACK;", ok=False)
+    c("V212-G2", "nota AR Bio apontando configuração fiscal da FSF: integridade de tenant real recusa", True, r.startswith("ERR"))
+    H.V212_DETAIL["G2"] = r[:300]
+
+    r = pg.sql(as_user(UF) + f"SELECT count(*) FROM public.fiscal_invoices WHERE organization_id='{ARB}';", ok=False)
+    c("V212-G3", "gestor FSF não lê notas da AR Bio (policies reais)", "0", val(r) if not r.startswith("ERR") else "ERR")
+
+    r = pg.sql("BEGIN; " + as_user(UF) + f"UPDATE public.fiscal_invoices SET invoice_type='NFSE' WHERE id='{FI_A}'; ROLLBACK;", ok=False)
+    pg.sql("ROLLBACK;", ok=False)
+    c("V212-G4", "gestor FSF não altera nota da AR Bio; tipo permanece", "NFE",
+      q(f"SELECT invoice_type FROM public.fiscal_invoices WHERE id='{FI_A}'"))
+
+    r = pg.sql("BEGIN; SET LOCAL ROLE anon; SELECT count(*) FROM public.bank_accounts; ROLLBACK;", ok=False)
+    pg.sql("ROLLBACK;", ok=False)
+    c("V212-G5", "anon sem privilégio em bank_accounts (ACL real só postgres/service_role)", True, "permission denied" in r)
+
+    r = pg.sql("BEGIN; SET LOCAL ROLE authenticated; SELECT count(*) FROM public.bank_accounts; ROLLBACK;", ok=False)
+    pg.sql("ROLLBACK;", ok=False)
+    c("V212-G6", "authenticated sem privilégio em bank_accounts (ACL real)", True, "permission denied" in r)
+
+    tg = q("SELECT count(*) FROM pg_trigger g JOIN pg_class t ON t.oid=g.tgrelid WHERE t.relname='fiscal_invoices' AND NOT g.tgisinternal AND g.tgenabled='O'")
+    c("V212-G7", "gatilhos reais de fiscal_invoices instalados e habilitados durante os cenários", "12", tg)

+"""Fechamento v2.12 (captura READ ONLY de produção 02/11, SHA-256 6b8fc306…8b6d): 21 relações, 450 colunas, 200 constraints,
+70 policies, 76 gatilhos (com corpo/MD5 da função), ACL e flags RLS reais, fn_fiscal_base_components_valid(jsonb) e a view
+vw_os_integridade_esteira. Sem linhas de dados.
+
+Integração sem stub/simulação:
+- 6 relações novas (fiscal_invoices, fiscal_document_series, payable_payments, financial_documents, financial_receipts,
+  financial_report_exports) entram pela MESMA via das extras reais (extras_schema.X);
+- as 15 relações já integradas exigem colunas/constraints IDÊNTICAS entre capturas (divergência aborta);
+- segurança das 21: RLS/FORCE reais, policies verbatim (as 7 já existentes nas extras devem ser idênticas), gatilhos
+  verbatim com a função do próprio anexo (MD5 de pg_get_functiondef conferido) e ACL de tabela reconstruída de relacl.
+"""
+import hashlib, json
+import extras_schema as XS
+
+PATH = XS.ROOT / "docs/auditorias/evidencias/catalogo-exclusivo-tenant-20261101/captura-v212-seguranca-20261102/fechamento-v212-seguranca-20261102.json"
+SHA = "6b8fc30604136116404d713b0b4fc4bef2cc16c2bdafccfb1fbe006d1f5a8b6d"
+assert hashlib.sha256(PATH.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == SHA, "captura v2.12 divergente"
+D = json.loads(PATH.read_text())
+assert tuple(len(D[k]) for k in ("relations", "columns", "constraints", "policies", "triggers", "functions", "views")) == (21, 450, 200, 70, 76, 1, 1)
+assert all(t["enabled"] == "O" for t in D["triggers"])
+
+# v2.13: public.fiscal_certificates (captura READ ONLY produção 02/11, SOMENTE esquema, sem linhas/certificados/segredos).
+# Convertida verbatim para o formato v2.12: colunas, constraints, policy, 2 gatilhos (MD5 do corpo conferido), ACL de
+# information_schema.table_privileges reconstruída em letras relacl e índices não-constraint executados verbatim.
+PATH13 = XS.ROOT / "docs/auditorias/evidencias/catalogo-exclusivo-tenant-20261101/captura-v213-fiscal-certificates-20261102/fiscal-certificates-schema-20261102.json"
+SHA13 = "b0c4265b1b5b685d0a475816fcc8923aecf34ef0478d00019e31653ede6bab84"
+assert hashlib.sha256(PATH13.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == SHA13, "captura v2.13 divergente"
+D13 = json.loads(PATH13.read_text())
+assert (len(D13["columns"]), len(D13["constraints"]), len(D13["policies"]), len(D13["triggers"]), len(D13["grants"]), len(D13["indexes"])) == (22, 6, 1, 2, 28, 3)
+_L = {v: k for k, v in {"r": "SELECT", "a": "INSERT", "w": "UPDATE", "d": "DELETE", "D": "TRUNCATE", "x": "REFERENCES", "t": "TRIGGER"}.items()}
+_acl = {}
+for g in D13["grants"]:
+    assert (g["table_schema"], g["table_name"], g["grantor"]) == ("public", "fiscal_certificates", "postgres")
+    _acl.setdefault(g["grantee"], "")
+    _acl[g["grantee"]] += _L[g["privilege_type"]] + ("*" if g["is_grantable"] == "YES" else "")
+D["relations"].append(dict(oid=None, nspname="public", relname="fiscal_certificates", relacl=[f"{k}={v}/postgres" for k, v in _acl.items()],
+                           relrowsecurity=D13["rls"]["enabled"], relforcerowsecurity=D13["rls"]["forced"]))
+D["columns"] += [dict(name=c["name"], type=c["type"], table="fiscal_certificates", schema="public", default=c["default"],
+                      identity="", generated="", not_null=c["not_null"]) for c in D13["columns"]]
+D["constraints"] += [dict(name=c["name"], table="fiscal_certificates", schema="public", definition=c["definition"]) for c in D13["constraints"]]
+D["policies"] += D13["policies"]
+for t in D13["triggers"]:
+    assert hashlib.md5(t["function"].encode()).hexdigest() == t["md5"], t["name"]
+    D["triggers"].append(dict(name=t["name"], table="fiscal_certificates", schema="public", enabled="O", definition=t["definition"],
+                              function_md5=t["md5"], function_definition=t["function"]))
+INDEXES13 = [i for i in D13["indexes"] if " fiscal_certificates_pkey " not in i]   # pkey já vem da constraint
+RELS = {(r["nspname"], r["relname"]): r for r in D["relations"]}
+PRIV = {"r": "SELECT", "a": "INSERT", "w": "UPDATE", "d": "DELETE", "D": "TRUNCATE", "x": "REFERENCES", "t": "TRIGGER", "m": "MAINTAIN"}
+CMD = {"ALL": "*", "SELECT": "r", "INSERT": "a", "UPDATE": "w", "DELETE": "d"}
+KIND = (("PRIMARY KEY", "p"), ("UNIQUE", "u"), ("FOREIGN KEY", "f"), ("CHECK", "c"), ("EXCLUDE", "x"))
+
+
+def _kind(defn):
+    for pre, k in KIND:
+        if defn.startswith(pre): return k
+    raise AssertionError("tipo de constraint desconhecido: " + defn[:60])
+
+
+def _pol(p):
+    return dict(name=p["policyname"], schema_name=p["schemaname"], table_name=p["tablename"], command=CMD[p["cmd"]],
+                permissive=p["permissive"] == "PERMISSIVE", roles=list(p["roles"]), using=p["qual"], check=p["with_check"])
+
+
+def merge():
+    """Chamar DEPOIS de v211_deps.merge(): acrescenta 6 relações, confere as 15 e aplica flags/policies reais. Idempotente."""
+    if XS.X.get("_v212"): return XS.X["_v212"]
+    have = {(r["nspname"], r["relname"]): r for r in XS.X["relations"]}
+    overlap, new = [k for k in RELS if k in have], [k for k in RELS if k not in have]
+    for s, t in overlap:
+        old = [(c["column_name"], c["type_sql"], c["default_expression"] or "", c["not_null"])
+               for c in sorted(XS.X["columns"], key=lambda c: c["position"]) if (c["schema_name"], c["table_name"]) == (s, t)]
+        nw = [(c["name"], c["type"], c["default"] or "", c["not_null"]) for c in D["columns"] if (c["schema"], c["table"]) == (s, t)]
+        assert old == nw, f"{s}.{t}: colunas divergentes entre capturas"
+        oc = {c["name"]: c["definition"] + ("" if c["validated"] else " NOT VALID") for c in XS.X["constraints"] if (c["schema_name"], c["table_name"]) == (s, t)}
+        nc = {c["name"]: c["definition"] for c in D["constraints"] if (c["schema"], c["table"]) == (s, t)}
+        assert oc == nc, f"{s}.{t}: constraints divergentes entre capturas"
+    pos = {}
+    for c in D["columns"]:
+        k = (c["schema"], c["table"])
+        if k in overlap: continue
+        pos[k] = pos.get(k, 0) + 1
+        XS.X["columns"].append(dict(schema_name=k[0], table_name=k[1], column_name=c["name"], type_sql=c["type"],
+                                    default_expression=c["default"] or None, identity=c["identity"], generated=c["generated"],
+                                    not_null=c["not_null"], position=pos[k]))
+    for c in D["constraints"]:
+        if (c["schema"], c["table"]) in overlap: continue
+        XS.X["constraints"].append(dict(name=c["name"], type=_kind(c["definition"]), validated="NOT VALID" not in c["definition"],
+                                        definition=c["definition"].replace(" NOT VALID", ""), table_name=c["table"], schema_name=c["schema"]))
+    for k in new:
+        XS.X["relations"].append(dict(oid=None, nspname=k[0], relkind="r", relname=k[1]))
+    for r in XS.X["relations"]:   # flags reais substituem rls_unknown
+        k = (r["nspname"], r["relname"])
+        if k in RELS:
+            assert r.get("rls_unknown") or r.get("relrowsecurity") is None or r["relrowsecurity"] == RELS[k]["relrowsecurity"], k
+            r.update(relrowsecurity=RELS[k]["relrowsecurity"], relforcerowsecurity=RELS[k]["relforcerowsecurity"], rls_unknown=False)
+    xp = {(p["schema_name"], p["table_name"], p["name"]): p for p in XS.X["policies"]}
+    same, added = [], []
+    for p in D["policies"]:
+        np_ = _pol(p); k = (np_["schema_name"], np_["table_name"], np_["name"])
+        if k in xp:
+            o = xp[k]
+            assert (o["command"], o["permissive"], sorted(o["roles"]), o["using"], o["check"]) == \
+                   (np_["command"], np_["permissive"], sorted(np_["roles"]), np_["using"], np_["check"]), f"policy divergente {k}"
+            same.append(k[2])
+        else:
+            XS.X["policies"].append(np_); added.append(k[2])
+    XS.X["_v212"] = dict(new_relations=[f"{a}.{b}" for a, b in new], identical_overlap=len(overlap),
+                         policies_identical=len(same), policies_added=len(added))
+    return XS.X["_v212"]
+
+
+def function():
+    f = D["functions"][0]
+    return dict(schema=f["schema"], name=f["name"], args="_c jsonb", definition=f["definition"], md5=f["md5"])
+
+
+def view_sql():
+    v = D["views"][0]
+    return v["name"], f"CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW {XS.fq(v['schema'], v['name'])} AS {v['definition']}"
+
+
+def trigger_functions():
+    """Funções distintas dos gatilhos: (nome qualificado, definição, md5)."""
+    out = {}
+    for t in D["triggers"]:
+        fn = t["function_definition"].split("(", 1)[0].split()[-1]
+        if fn in out: assert out[fn][1] == t["function_md5"], f"md5 divergente para {fn}"
+        out[fn] = (t["function_definition"], t["function_md5"])
+    return out
+
+
+def triggers():
+    return [(t["schema"], t["table"], t["name"], t["definition"]) for t in D["triggers"]]
+
+
+def acl_sql(server_major):
+    """REVOKE ALL dos papéis clientes + GRANT exato de relacl. MAINTAIN só existe no PG17+: declarado se ausente."""
+    out, dropped = [], []
+    for (s, t), r in RELS.items():
+        rel = XS.fq(s, t)
+        out.append(f"REVOKE ALL ON {rel} FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;")
+        for a in r["relacl"] or []:
+            who, rest = a.split("=", 1); privs = rest.split("/")[0]
+            if who in ("postgres", ""): continue
+            ps = []
+            for ch in privs.replace("*", ""):
+                if ch == "m" and server_major < 17: dropped.append(f"{s}.{t}:{who}:MAINTAIN"); continue
+                ps.append(PRIV[ch])
+            if ps: out.append(f"GRANT {', '.join(ps)} ON {rel} TO {XS.q(who)};")
+    return out, dropped
+
+
+def acl_expected():
+    """{(tabela, papel): set(letras)} sem MAINTAIN para conferência via has_table_privilege."""
+    exp = {}
+    for (s, t), r in RELS.items():
+        for a in r["relacl"] or []:
+            who, rest = a.split("=", 1)
+            if who in ("postgres", ""): continue
+            exp[(f"{s}.{t}", who)] = set(rest.split("/")[0].replace("*", "")) - {"m"}
+    return exp

+-- 00 PRE GLOBAL — catálogo exclusivo por tenant. NO_GO. Somente leitura (READ ONLY).
+BEGIN TRANSACTION READ ONLY;
+DO $pre$
+DECLARE v_fail text[] := ARRAY[]::text[]; r record; v_n bigint;
+BEGIN
+  -- 1. FKs reais para produtos devem estar todas mapeadas (e vice-versa)
+  FOR r IN
+    SELECT c.conrelid::regclass::text AS rel, a.attname AS col
+      FROM pg_constraint c JOIN pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid=c.conrelid AND a.attnum = ANY(c.conkey)
+     WHERE c.contype='f' AND c.confrelid='public.produtos'::regclass
+  LOOP
+    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM (VALUES ('entregas','produto_id','reference'),('lotes','produto_id','reference'),('ordens_servico','produto_id','reference'),('programacao_producao','produto_id','reference'),('catalog_tenant_visibility','produto_id','reference'),('commercial_condition_item_prices','product_id','reference'),('commercial_price_rules','product_id','reference'),('partner_product_prices','product_id','reference'),('partner_order_items','product_id','reference'),('partner_simulation_items','product_id','reference'),('estoque_produto','produto_id','reference'),('estoque_embalagens','produto_id','reference'),('produto_etiquetas','produto_id','reference'),('os_faturamento_base','produto_id','reference'),('ordens_servico_onfarm','produto_id','reference'),('registros_consultor_recomendacoes','produto_biologico_sugerido_id','reference'),('commercial_campaign_products','product_id','reference'),('product_fiscal_profiles','product_id','descendant'),('product_technical_profiles','produto_id','descendant'),('produto_composicao_conjuntos','produto_id','descendant'),('produto_conformidade_perfis','produto_id','descendant'),('product_crop_recommendations','produto_id','descendant'),('product_target_recommendations','produto_id','descendant'),('cq_parametros_referencia','produto_id','descendant'),('catalogo_volumes','produto_id','descendant'),('formulacoes','produto_id','descendant'),('formulacoes','component_insumo_id','reference'),('microrganismos','product_id','nullify')) m(t,c,k)
+                   WHERE ('public.'||m.t = r.rel OR m.t = r.rel) AND m.c = r.col) THEN
+      v_fail := v_fail || ('fk_unmapped:'||r.rel||'.'||r.col);
+    END IF;
+  END LOOP;
+  FOR r IN SELECT * FROM (VALUES ('entregas','produto_id','reference'),('lotes','produto_id','reference'),('ordens_servico','produto_id','reference'),('programacao_producao','produto_id','reference'),('catalog_tenant_visibility','produto_id','reference'),('commercial_condition_item_prices','product_id','reference'),('commercial_price_rules','product_id','reference'),('partner_product_prices','product_id','reference'),('partner_order_items','product_id','reference'),('partner_simulation_items','product_id','reference'),('estoque_produto','produto_id','reference'),('estoque_embalagens','produto_id','reference'),('produto_etiquetas','produto_id','reference'),('os_faturamento_base','produto_id','reference'),('ordens_servico_onfarm','produto_id','reference'),('registros_consultor_recomendacoes','produto_biologico_sugerido_id','reference'),('commercial_campaign_products','product_id','reference'),('product_fiscal_profiles','product_id','descendant'),('product_technical_profiles','produto_id','descendant'),('produto_composicao_conjuntos','produto_id','descendant'),('produto_conformidade_perfis','produto_id','descendant'),('product_crop_recommendations','produto_id','descendant'),('product_target_recommendations','produto_id','descendant'),('cq_parametros_referencia','produto_id','descendant'),('catalogo_volumes','produto_id','descendant'),('formulacoes','produto_id','descendant'),('formulacoes','component_insumo_id','reference'),('microrganismos','product_id','nullify')) m(t,c,k) LOOP
+    IF to_regclass('public.'||r.t) IS NULL THEN v_fail := v_fail || ('table_missing:'||r.t); CONTINUE; END IF;
+    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_attribute WHERE attrelid=('public.'||r.t)::regclass AND attname=r.c AND NOT attisdropped)
+      THEN v_fail := v_fail || ('column_missing:'||r.t||'.'||r.c); END IF;
+  END LOOP;
+  -- 2. estado anterior: catálogo exclusivo ainda não instalado e fundação opcional antiga ausente
+  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_attribute WHERE attrelid='public.produtos'::regclass AND attname='owner_organization_id' AND NOT attisdropped)
+    THEN v_fail := v_fail || 'partial:produtos.owner_organization_id'::text; END IF;
+  IF to_regclass('platform_private.tenant_catalog_product_map') IS NOT NULL THEN v_fail := v_fail || 'partial:map_present'::text; END IF;
+  IF to_regprocedure('public.tenant_product_save(uuid,jsonb,timestamptz,uuid)') IS NOT NULL THEN v_fail := v_fail || 'partial:rpc_present'::text; END IF;
+  -- 3. dependências canônicas
+  IF to_regprocedure('platform_private.catalog_tenant_actor(text)') IS NULL THEN v_fail := v_fail || 'dep:catalog_tenant_actor'::text; END IF;
+  IF to_regclass('public.organizations') IS NULL OR to_regclass('public.partner_accounts') IS NULL THEN v_fail := v_fail || 'dep:organizations/partner_accounts'::text; END IF;
+  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_attribute WHERE attrelid='public.partner_accounts'::regclass AND attname='commercial_owner_organization_id')
+    THEN v_fail := v_fail || 'dep:partner_accounts.commercial_owner_organization_id'::text; END IF;
+  -- 4. índices únicos de produtos por expressão/parciais não são reescritos automaticamente
+  FOR r IN SELECT i.indexrelid::regclass::text AS ix FROM pg_index i
+            WHERE i.indrelid='public.produtos'::regclass AND i.indisunique AND NOT i.indisprimary
+              AND (i.indexprs IS NOT NULL OR i.indpred IS NOT NULL) LOOP
+    v_fail := v_fail || ('unique_expr_or_partial:'||r.ix);
+  END LOOP;
+  -- 5. microrganismos.product_id precisa aceitar NULL para anulação registrada
+  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_attribute WHERE attrelid='public.microrganismos'::regclass AND attname='product_id' AND attnotnull)
+    THEN v_fail := v_fail || 'nullify_not_nullable:microrganismos.product_id'::text; END IF;
+  IF cardinality(v_fail) > 0 THEN
+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_catalog_pre_failed' USING DETAIL = array_to_string(v_fail, ' | ');
+  END IF;
+END $pre$;
+COMMIT;

+-- 01 MIGRAÇÃO TRANSACIONAL — catálogo EXCLUSIVO por tenant. NO_GO: NÃO APLICAR sem revisão e ordem explícita.
+-- Dados de teste autorizados (01/11/2026): referências repontadas para a cópia do tenant do registro.

+SET LOCAL statement_timeout = '15min';

+DECLARE v_fail text[] := ARRAY[]::text[]; r record; v_n bigint;
+BEGIN
+  -- 1. FKs reais para produtos devem estar todas mapeadas (e vice-versa)
+  FOR r IN

+    IF to_regclass('public.'||r.t) IS NULL THEN v_fail := v_fail || ('table_missing:'||r.t); CONTINUE; END IF;
+    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_attribute WHERE attrelid=('public.'||r.t)::regclass AND attname=r.c AND NOT attisdropped)
+      THEN v_fail := v_fail || ('column_missing:'||r.t||'.'||r.c); END IF;
+  END LOOP;
+  -- 2. estado anterior: catálogo exclusivo ainda não instalado e fundação opcional antiga ausente
+  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_attribute WHERE attrelid='public.produtos'::regclass AND attname='owner_organization_id' AND NOT attisdropped)
+    THEN v_fail := v_fail || 'partial:produtos.owner_organization_id'::text; END IF;
+  IF to_regclass('platform_private.tenant_catalog_product_map') IS NOT NULL THEN v_fail := v_fail || 'partial:map_present'::text; END IF;
+  IF to_regprocedure('public.tenant_product_save(uuid,jsonb,timestamptz,uuid)') IS NOT NULL THEN v_fail := v_fail || 'partial:rpc_present'::text; END IF;

+ALTER TABLE public.produtos ADD COLUMN owner_organization_id uuid REFERENCES public.organizations(id);
+DO $ix$ DECLARE r record; v_cols text; BEGIN
+  FOR r IN SELECT i.indexrelid, i.indexrelid::regclass::text AS ix, c.conname
+             FROM pg_index i LEFT JOIN pg_constraint c ON c.conindid = i.indexrelid
+            WHERE i.indrelid='public.produtos'::regclass AND i.indisunique AND NOT i.indisprimary LOOP
+    SELECT string_agg(quote_ident(a.attname), ', ' ORDER BY k.ord) INTO v_cols
+      FROM pg_index i2, unnest(i2.indkey) WITH ORDINALITY k(attnum, ord) JOIN pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid='public.produtos'::regclass AND a.attnum=k.attnum
+     WHERE i2.indexrelid = r.indexrelid;
+    INSERT INTO platform_private.tenant_catalog_migration_ledger(step, detail)
+      VALUES ('unique_rescoped', jsonb_build_object('index', r.ix, 'def', pg_get_indexdef(r.indexrelid), 'cols', v_cols));
+    IF r.conname IS NOT NULL THEN EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE public.produtos DROP CONSTRAINT %I', r.conname);
+    ELSE EXECUTE format('DROP INDEX %s', r.ix); END IF;
+    EXECUTE format('CREATE UNIQUE INDEX %I ON public.produtos (owner_organization_id, %s)', r.ix::text || '_tenant', v_cols);
+  END LOOP;
+END $ix$;
+INSERT INTO public.produtos

+    FROM public.produtos p JOIN platform_private.tenant_catalog_product_map m ON m.old_product_id = p.id;
+DO $d$ DECLARE v_has_id boolean := EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_attribute WHERE attrelid='public.product_fiscal_profiles'::regclass AND attname='id' AND NOT attisdropped);

+      FROM public.product_fiscal_profiles s JOIN platform_private.tenant_catalog_product_map m ON m.old_product_id = s.product_id;

+  INSERT INTO platform_private.tenant_catalog_migration_ledger(step, detail) VALUES ('descendant_copied', jsonb_build_object('table','product_fiscal_profiles','rows',v_n));
+END $d$;
+DO $d$ DECLARE v_has_id boolean := EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_attribute WHERE attrelid='public.product_technical_profiles'::regclass AND attname='id' AND NOT attisdropped);

+  UPDATE public.commercial_campaign_products x SET product_id = (SELECT m.new_product_id FROM platform_private.tenant_catalog_product_map m
+                                  WHERE m.old_product_id = x.product_id AND m.organization_id = NULL::uuid)
+   WHERE x.product_id IN (SELECT id FROM public.produtos WHERE owner_organization_id IS NULL);
+  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_n = ROW_COUNT;
+  SELECT count(*) INTO v_left FROM public.commercial_campaign_products x WHERE x.product_id IS NOT NULL
+     AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.produtos p WHERE p.id = x.product_id AND p.owner_organization_id IS NOT NULL);
+  IF v_left > 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_catalog_reference_unresolved' USING DETAIL = 'commercial_campaign_products.product_id='||v_left; END IF;
+  INSERT INTO platform_private.tenant_catalog_migration_ledger(step, detail) VALUES ('reference_repointed', jsonb_build_object('table','commercial_campaign_products','column','product_id','rows',v_n));
+END $r$;
+DO $r$ DECLARE v_n bigint; v_left bigint; BEGIN
+  UPDATE public.formulacoes x SET component_insumo_id = (SELECT m.new_product_id FROM platform_private.tenant_catalog_product_map m
+                                  WHERE m.old_product_id = x.component_insumo_id AND m.organization_id = (SELECT p.owner_organization_id FROM public.produtos p WHERE p.id = x.produto_id))
+   WHERE x.component_insumo_id IN (SELECT id FROM public.produtos WHERE owner_organization_id IS NULL);
+  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_n = ROW_COUNT;
+  SELECT count(*) INTO v_left FROM public.formulacoes x WHERE x.component_insumo_id IS NOT NULL
+     AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.produtos p WHERE p.id = x.component_insumo_id AND p.owner_organization_id IS NOT NULL);
+  IF v_left > 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_catalog_reference_unresolved' USING DETAIL = 'formulacoes.component_insumo_id='||v_left; END IF;
+  INSERT INTO platform_private.tenant_catalog_migration_ledger(step, detail) VALUES ('reference_repointed', jsonb_build_object('table','formulacoes','column','component_insumo_id','rows',v_n));
+END $r$;
+INSERT INTO platform_private.tenant_catalog_migration_ledger(step, detail)
+  SELECT 'reference_nullified', jsonb_build_object('table','microrganismos','column','product_id','ids', coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', id, 'old', product_id)),'[]'))
+    FROM public.microrganismos WHERE product_id IS NOT NULL;
+UPDATE public.microrganismos SET product_id = NULL WHERE product_id IS NOT NULL;
+INSERT INTO platform_private.tenant_catalog_migration_ledger(step, detail)
+  SELECT 'global_removed', jsonb_build_object('count', count(*), 'unused', count(*) FILTER (WHERE NOT EXISTS
+     (SELECT 1 FROM platform_private.tenant_catalog_product_map m WHERE m.old_product_id = p.id)),
+     'snapshot', coalesce(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(p)), '[]'))
+    FROM public.produtos p WHERE p.owner_organization_id IS NULL;
+DELETE FROM public.produtos WHERE owner_organization_id IS NULL;
+ALTER TABLE public.produtos ALTER COLUMN owner_organization_id SET NOT NULL;
+DO $dup$ DECLARE v text; BEGIN
+  SELECT string_agg(owner_organization_id::text||':'||k, ', ') INTO v FROM (
+    SELECT owner_organization_id, lower(btrim(nome)) k FROM public.produtos GROUP BY 1, 2 HAVING count(*) > 1) d;
+  IF v IS NOT NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_catalog_duplicate_name' USING DETAIL = v; END IF;
+END $dup$;
+CREATE UNIQUE INDEX produtos_owner_nome_uniq ON public.produtos (owner_organization_id, lower(btrim(nome)));
+CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS produtos_owner_idx ON public.produtos (owner_organization_id);
+CREATE FUNCTION platform_private.produtos_owner_immutable() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql SET search_path = '' AS $f$
+BEGIN
+  IF NEW.owner_organization_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.owner_organization_id OR NEW.id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.id THEN
+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_owner_immutable' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
+  END IF;
+  RETURN NEW;
+END $f$;
+CREATE TRIGGER a00_produtos_owner_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON public.produtos
+  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform_private.produtos_owner_immutable();
+DO $t$ DECLARE r record; BEGIN
+  FOR r IN SELECT * FROM tc_triggers LOOP
+    EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE %s %s TRIGGER %I', r.rel,
+      CASE r.tgenabled WHEN 'A' THEN 'ENABLE ALWAYS' WHEN 'R' THEN 'ENABLE REPLICA' ELSE 'ENABLE' END, r.tgname);
+  END LOOP;
+END $t$;
+CREATE FUNCTION platform_private.tenant_catalog_read_org() RETURNS uuid LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = '' AS $f$
+BEGIN
+  RETURN platform_private.catalog_tenant_actor('read');
+EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN RETURN NULL;
+END $f$;
+REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION platform_private.tenant_catalog_read_org() FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;
+GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION platform_private.tenant_catalog_read_org() TO authenticated;
+GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA platform_private TO authenticated;
+INSERT INTO platform_private.tenant_catalog_migration_ledger(step, detail)
+  SELECT 'policies_replaced', coalesce(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('name', policyname, 'cmd', cmd, 'qual', qual, 'check', with_check)), '[]')
+    FROM pg_policies WHERE schemaname='public' AND tablename='produtos';
+DO $p$ DECLARE r record; BEGIN
+  FOR r IN SELECT policyname FROM pg_policies WHERE schemaname='public' AND tablename='produtos' LOOP
+    EXECUTE format('DROP POLICY %I ON public.produtos', r.policyname);
+  END LOOP;
+END $p$;
+ALTER TABLE public.produtos ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

+GRANT SELECT ON public.produtos TO authenticated;

+  USING (owner_organization_id = platform_private.tenant_catalog_read_org());

+-- leitura permissiva explícita: sem ela, tabela antes sem RLS ficaria invisível ao próprio tenant
+CREATE POLICY tenant_catalog_owner_read ON public.catalogo_volumes FOR SELECT TO authenticated
+  USING (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.produtos p WHERE p.id = catalogo_volumes.produto_id));

+  USING (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.produtos p WHERE p.id = cq_parametros_referencia.produto_id));

+CREATE TABLE public.tenant_product_receipts(
+  actor_id uuid NOT NULL, organization_id uuid NOT NULL, idempotency_key uuid NOT NULL, payload_hash text NOT NULL,
+  product_id uuid NOT NULL, result jsonb NOT NULL, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
+  PRIMARY KEY (actor_id, organization_id, idempotency_key));
+CREATE TABLE public.tenant_product_audit(
+  id bigserial PRIMARY KEY, organization_id uuid NOT NULL, product_id uuid NOT NULL, actor_id uuid NOT NULL,
+  action text NOT NULL, before_state jsonb, after_state jsonb NOT NULL, at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now());
+REVOKE ALL ON public.tenant_product_receipts, public.tenant_product_audit FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
+GRANT ALL ON public.tenant_product_receipts, public.tenant_product_audit TO service_role;
+GRANT SELECT ON public.tenant_product_audit TO authenticated;
+ALTER TABLE public.tenant_product_receipts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
+ALTER TABLE public.tenant_product_audit ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
+CREATE POLICY tenant_product_audit_select ON public.tenant_product_audit FOR SELECT TO authenticated
+  USING (organization_id = platform_private.tenant_catalog_read_org());
+CREATE FUNCTION platform_private.tenant_product_audit_immutable() RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql SET search_path = '' AS $f$
+BEGIN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_audit_immutable' USING ERRCODE = '42501'; END $f$;
+CREATE TRIGGER a00_tenant_product_audit_immutable BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON public.tenant_product_audit
+  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION platform_private.tenant_product_audit_immutable();
+
+CREATE FUNCTION platform_private.tenant_product_validate(p_payload jsonb, p_create boolean) RETURNS void
+LANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE SET search_path = '' AS $f$
+DECLARE k text; v_num numeric;
+BEGIN
+  IF p_payload IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(p_payload) <> 'object' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_payload_invalid' USING ERRCODE='22023'; END IF;
+  FOR k IN SELECT jsonb_object_keys(p_payload) LOOP
+    IF NOT k = ANY (ARRAY['nome', 'codigo', 'descricao', 'tipo', 'classe_catalogo', 'familia_catalogo', 'modo_acao', 'modo_aplicacao', 'funcao_completa', 'e_insumo', 'b2b_enabled', 'dose_ml_ha', 'estoque_minimo', 'valor_dose_brl', 'valor_ha_brl', 'valor_litro_brl']) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_field_forbidden:%', k USING ERRCODE='22023'; END IF;
+  END LOOP;
+  IF p_create AND NOT p_payload ? 'nome' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_nome_required' USING ERRCODE='22023'; END IF;
+  IF p_payload ? 'nome' AND (jsonb_typeof(p_payload->'nome') <> 'string' OR char_length(btrim(p_payload->>'nome')) NOT BETWEEN 2 AND 200) THEN
+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_nome_invalid' USING ERRCODE='22023'; END IF;
+  FOREACH k IN ARRAY ARRAY['dose_ml_ha','estoque_minimo','valor_dose_brl','valor_ha_brl','valor_litro_brl'] LOOP
+    IF p_payload ? k AND jsonb_typeof(p_payload->k) <> 'null' THEN
+      IF jsonb_typeof(p_payload->k) <> 'number' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_number_invalid:%', k USING ERRCODE='22023'; END IF;
+      v_num := (p_payload->>k)::numeric;
+      IF v_num < 0 OR v_num > 1e12 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_number_out_of_range:%', k USING ERRCODE='22023'; END IF;
+    END IF;
+  END LOOP;
+  FOREACH k IN ARRAY ARRAY['e_insumo','b2b_enabled'] LOOP
+    IF p_payload ? k AND jsonb_typeof(p_payload->k) NOT IN ('boolean','null') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_boolean_invalid:%', k USING ERRCODE='22023'; END IF;
+  END LOOP;
+END $f$;
+
+CREATE FUNCTION public.tenant_product_save(p_product_id uuid, p_payload jsonb, p_expected_updated_at timestamptz, p_idempotency_key uuid)
+RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = '' AS $f$
+DECLARE v_uid uuid := auth.uid(); v_org uuid; v_org2 uuid; v_hash text; v_rc public.tenant_product_receipts%ROWTYPE;
+  v_prev public.produtos%ROWTYPE; v_new public.produtos%ROWTYPE; v_res jsonb;
+BEGIN
+  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'unauthenticated' USING ERRCODE='28000'; END IF;
+  v_org := platform_private.catalog_tenant_actor('mutate');          -- tenant derivado do servidor
+  IF p_idempotency_key IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_key_required' USING ERRCODE='22023'; END IF;
+  PERFORM platform_private.tenant_product_validate(p_payload, p_product_id IS NULL);
+  v_hash := md5(concat_ws('|', coalesce(p_product_id::text,'new'), p_payload::text, coalesce(p_expected_updated_at::text,'')));
+  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(concat_ws(':','tpk',v_uid,v_org,p_idempotency_key),0));
+  SELECT * INTO v_rc FROM public.tenant_product_receipts WHERE actor_id=v_uid AND organization_id=v_org AND idempotency_key=p_idempotency_key;
+  IF FOUND THEN
+    IF v_rc.payload_hash <> v_hash THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_idempotency_conflict' USING ERRCODE='22023'; END IF;
+    RETURN v_rc.result || jsonb_build_object('replayed', true);
+  END IF;
+  IF p_product_id IS NULL THEN
+    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(concat_ws(':','tpn',v_org,lower(btrim(p_payload->>'nome'))),0));
+    v_org2 := platform_private.catalog_tenant_actor('mutate');         -- reautoriza após espera
+    IF v_org2 IS DISTINCT FROM v_org THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_scope_changed' USING ERRCODE='42501'; END IF;
+    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.produtos WHERE owner_organization_id=v_org AND lower(btrim(nome))=lower(btrim(p_payload->>'nome'))) THEN
+      RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_duplicate_name' USING ERRCODE='PT409'; END IF;
+    INSERT INTO public.produtos(id, owner_organization_id, ativo, nome, codigo, descricao, tipo, classe_catalogo, familia_catalogo, modo_acao, modo_aplicacao, funcao_completa, e_insumo, b2b_enabled, dose_ml_ha, estoque_minimo, valor_dose_brl, valor_ha_brl, valor_litro_brl)
+      VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), v_org, true, (p_payload->>'nome')::text, (p_payload->>'codigo')::text, (p_payload->>'descricao')::text, (p_payload->>'tipo')::text, (p_payload->>'classe_catalogo')::text, (p_payload->>'familia_catalogo')::text, (p_payload->>'modo_acao')::text, (p_payload->>'modo_aplicacao')::text, (p_payload->>'funcao_completa')::text, (p_payload->>'e_insumo')::boolean, (p_payload->>'b2b_enabled')::boolean, (p_payload->>'dose_ml_ha')::numeric, (p_payload->>'estoque_minimo')::numeric, (p_payload->>'valor_dose_brl')::numeric, (p_payload->>'valor_ha_brl')::numeric, (p_payload->>'valor_litro_brl')::numeric) RETURNING * INTO v_new;
+    INSERT INTO public.tenant_product_audit(organization_id, product_id, actor_id, action, before_state, after_state)
+      VALUES (v_org, v_new.id, v_uid, 'create', NULL, to_jsonb(v_new));
+  ELSE
+    IF p_expected_updated_at IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_cas_required' USING ERRCODE='PT409'; END IF;
+    SELECT * INTO v_prev FROM public.produtos WHERE id=p_product_id AND owner_organization_id=v_org FOR UPDATE;
+    IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_not_found' USING ERRCODE='P0002'; END IF;
+    v_org2 := platform_private.catalog_tenant_actor('mutate');           -- reautoriza após lock
+    IF v_org2 IS DISTINCT FROM v_org THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_scope_changed' USING ERRCODE='42501'; END IF;
+    IF v_prev.updated_at IS DISTINCT FROM p_expected_updated_at THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_version_conflict' USING ERRCODE='PT409'; END IF;
+    IF p_payload ? 'nome' AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.produtos WHERE owner_organization_id=v_org AND id<>p_product_id
+         AND lower(btrim(nome))=lower(btrim(p_payload->>'nome'))) THEN
+      RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_duplicate_name' USING ERRCODE='PT409'; END IF;
+    UPDATE public.produtos SET
+      nome = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'nome' THEN (p_payload->>'nome')::text ELSE nome END,
+      codigo = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'codigo' THEN (p_payload->>'codigo')::text ELSE codigo END,
+      descricao = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'descricao' THEN (p_payload->>'descricao')::text ELSE descricao END,
+      tipo = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'tipo' THEN (p_payload->>'tipo')::text ELSE tipo END,
+      classe_catalogo = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'classe_catalogo' THEN (p_payload->>'classe_catalogo')::text ELSE classe_catalogo END,
+      familia_catalogo = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'familia_catalogo' THEN (p_payload->>'familia_catalogo')::text ELSE familia_catalogo END,
+      modo_acao = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'modo_acao' THEN (p_payload->>'modo_acao')::text ELSE modo_acao END,
+      modo_aplicacao = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'modo_aplicacao' THEN (p_payload->>'modo_aplicacao')::text ELSE modo_aplicacao END,
+      funcao_completa = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'funcao_completa' THEN (p_payload->>'funcao_completa')::text ELSE funcao_completa END,
+      e_insumo = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'e_insumo' THEN (p_payload->>'e_insumo')::boolean ELSE e_insumo END,
+      b2b_enabled = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'b2b_enabled' THEN (p_payload->>'b2b_enabled')::boolean ELSE b2b_enabled END,
+      dose_ml_ha = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'dose_ml_ha' THEN (p_payload->>'dose_ml_ha')::numeric ELSE dose_ml_ha END,
+      estoque_minimo = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'estoque_minimo' THEN (p_payload->>'estoque_minimo')::numeric ELSE estoque_minimo END,
+      valor_dose_brl = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'valor_dose_brl' THEN (p_payload->>'valor_dose_brl')::numeric ELSE valor_dose_brl END,
+      valor_ha_brl = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'valor_ha_brl' THEN (p_payload->>'valor_ha_brl')::numeric ELSE valor_ha_brl END,
+      valor_litro_brl = CASE WHEN p_payload ? 'valor_litro_brl' THEN (p_payload->>'valor_litro_brl')::numeric ELSE valor_litro_brl END,
+      updated_at = clock_timestamp()
+     WHERE id=p_product_id AND owner_organization_id=v_org AND updated_at = p_expected_updated_at
+     RETURNING * INTO v_new;
+    IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_version_conflict' USING ERRCODE='PT409'; END IF;
+    INSERT INTO public.tenant_product_audit(organization_id, product_id, actor_id, action, before_state, after_state)
+      VALUES (v_org, v_new.id, v_uid, 'update', to_jsonb(v_prev), to_jsonb(v_new));
+  END IF;
+  v_res := jsonb_build_object('product_id', v_new.id, 'organization_id', v_org, 'updated_at', v_new.updated_at, 'replayed', false);
+  INSERT INTO public.tenant_product_receipts VALUES (v_uid, v_org, p_idempotency_key, v_hash, v_new.id, v_res, now());
+  RETURN v_res;
+END $f$;
+
+CREATE FUNCTION public.tenant_product_set_archived(p_product_id uuid, p_archived boolean, p_expected_updated_at timestamptz, p_idempotency_key uuid)
+RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = '' AS $f$
+DECLARE v_uid uuid := auth.uid(); v_org uuid; v_org2 uuid; v_hash text; v_rc public.tenant_product_receipts%ROWTYPE;
+  v_prev public.produtos%ROWTYPE; v_new public.produtos%ROWTYPE; v_res jsonb;
+BEGIN
+  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'unauthenticated' USING ERRCODE='28000'; END IF;
+  v_org := platform_private.catalog_tenant_actor('mutate');
+  IF p_product_id IS NULL OR p_archived IS NULL OR p_idempotency_key IS NULL OR p_expected_updated_at IS NULL THEN
+    RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_payload_invalid' USING ERRCODE='22023'; END IF;
+  v_hash := md5(concat_ws('|','archive',p_product_id,p_archived,p_expected_updated_at));
+  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(concat_ws(':','tpk',v_uid,v_org,p_idempotency_key),0));
+  SELECT * INTO v_rc FROM public.tenant_product_receipts WHERE actor_id=v_uid AND organization_id=v_org AND idempotency_key=p_idempotency_key;
+  IF FOUND THEN
+    IF v_rc.payload_hash <> v_hash THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_idempotency_conflict' USING ERRCODE='22023'; END IF;
+    RETURN v_rc.result || jsonb_build_object('replayed', true);
+  END IF;
+  SELECT * INTO v_prev FROM public.produtos WHERE id=p_product_id AND owner_organization_id=v_org FOR UPDATE;
+  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_not_found' USING ERRCODE='P0002'; END IF;
+  v_org2 := platform_private.catalog_tenant_actor('mutate');
+  IF v_org2 IS DISTINCT FROM v_org THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_scope_changed' USING ERRCODE='42501'; END IF;
+  IF v_prev.updated_at IS DISTINCT FROM p_expected_updated_at THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_version_conflict' USING ERRCODE='PT409'; END IF;
+  IF (v_prev.ativo IS NOT TRUE) = p_archived THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_no_change' USING ERRCODE='22023'; END IF;
+  UPDATE public.produtos SET ativo = NOT p_archived, updated_at = clock_timestamp()
+   WHERE id=p_product_id AND owner_organization_id=v_org AND updated_at=p_expected_updated_at RETURNING * INTO v_new;
+  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'tenant_product_version_conflict' USING ERRCODE='PT409'; END IF;
+  INSERT INTO public.tenant_product_audit(organization_id, product_id, actor_id, action, before_state, after_state)
+    VALUES (v_org, v_new.id, v_uid, CASE WHEN p_archived THEN 'archive' ELSE 'unarchive' END, to_jsonb(v_prev), to_jsonb(v_new));
+  v_res := jsonb_build_object('product_id', v_new.id, 'ativo', v_new.ativo, 'updated_at', v_new.updated_at, 'replayed', false);
+  INSERT INTO public.tenant_product_receipts VALUES (v_uid, v_org, p_idempotency_key, v_hash, v_new.id, v_res, now());
+  RETURN v_res;
+END $f$;
+
+CREATE FUNCTION public.tenant_product_list(p_include_archived boolean DEFAULT false)
+RETURNS SETOF public.produtos LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = '' AS $f$
+DECLARE v_org uuid;
+BEGIN
+  IF auth.uid() IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'unauthenticated' USING ERRCODE='28000'; END IF;
+  v_org := platform_private.catalog_tenant_actor('read');
+  RETURN QUERY SELECT * FROM public.produtos WHERE owner_organization_id = v_org AND (p_include_archived OR ativo IS TRUE) ORDER BY nome;
+END $f$;
+DO $acl$ DECLARE f text; BEGIN
+  FOREACH f IN ARRAY ARRAY['public.tenant_product_save(uuid,jsonb,timestamptz,uuid)','public.tenant_product_set_archived(uuid,boolean,timestamptz,uuid)',
+                           'public.tenant_product_list(boolean)'] LOOP
+    EXECUTE format('REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION %s FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role', f);
+    EXECUTE format('GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION %s TO authenticated', f);
+  END LOOP;
+  FOREACH f IN ARRAY ARRAY['platform_private.tenant_product_validate(jsonb,boolean)','platform_private.produtos_owner_immutable()',
+                           'platform_private.tenant_product_audit_immutable()'] LOOP
+    EXECUTE format('REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION %s FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role', f);
+  END LOOP;
+END $acl$;
+DO $g$ DECLARE r record; BEGIN
+  FOR r IN SELECT p.oid::regprocedure AS sig FROM pg_proc p JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid=p.pronamespace
+            WHERE n.nspname IN ('public','platform_private') AND p.proname IN ('platform_create_global_product', 'platform_read_global_product_catalog', 'platform_read_global_product_catalog_by_family', 'platform_read_global_product_composition', 'platform_read_global_product_metadata', 'platform_set_global_product_composition', 'platform_set_global_product_family', 'platform_set_global_product_status', 'platform_update_global_product_metadata', 'platform_wipe_global_product_catalog') LOOP
+    EXECUTE format('REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION %s FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role', r.sig);
+    INSERT INTO platform_private.tenant_catalog_migration_ledger(step, detail) VALUES ('global_function_closed', jsonb_build_object('sig', r.sig::text));
+  END LOOP;
+END $g$;
+DO $post$ DECLARE v_n bigint; r record; BEGIN
+  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.produtos WHERE owner_organization_id IS NULL) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'post:owner_null'; END IF;
+  IF has_table_privilege('anon','public.produtos','SELECT') OR has_table_privilege('authenticated','public.produtos','INSERT')
+     OR has_table_privilege('authenticated','public.produtos','UPDATE') OR has_table_privilege('authenticated','public.produtos','DELETE')
+     OR has_table_privilege('authenticated','public.produtos','TRUNCATE') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'post:produtos_acl_open'; END IF;
+  FOR r IN SELECT p.oid::regprocedure AS sig FROM pg_proc p JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid=p.pronamespace
+            WHERE n.nspname IN ('public','platform_private') AND p.proname IN ('platform_create_global_product', 'platform_read_global_product_catalog', 'platform_read_global_product_catalog_by_family', 'platform_read_global_product_composition', 'platform_read_global_product_metadata', 'platform_set_global_product_composition', 'platform_set_global_product_family', 'platform_set_global_product_status', 'platform_update_global_product_metadata', 'platform_wipe_global_product_catalog') LOOP
+    IF has_function_privilege('authenticated', r.sig, 'EXECUTE') OR has_function_privilege('anon', r.sig, 'EXECUTE') THEN
+      RAISE EXCEPTION 'post:global_function_open:%', r.sig; END IF;
+  END LOOP;
+  IF has_function_privilege('anon','public.tenant_product_save(uuid,jsonb,timestamptz,uuid)','EXECUTE') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'post:rpc_anon'; END IF;
+  SELECT count(*) INTO v_n FROM tc_triggers t JOIN pg_trigger g ON g.tgname=t.tgname AND g.tgrelid=t.rel::regclass WHERE g.tgenabled <> t.tgenabled;
+  IF v_n > 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'post:trigger_state_not_restored'; END IF;
+  INSERT INTO platform_private.tenant_catalog_migration_ledger(step, detail) VALUES ('post_ok', jsonb_build_object('products', (SELECT count(*) FROM public.produtos)));
+END $post$;
+COMMIT;
